    function renderRail() { const items = [['files', 'file', 'Files'], ['outline', 'outline', 'Outline'], ['search', 'search', 'Search'], ['insert', 'plus', 'Insert'], ['assets', 'image', 'Media'], ['references', 'refs', 'References'], ['diagnostics', 'warning', 'Checks']]; const bottom = [['history', 'history', 'History'], ['templates', 'template', 'Templates'], ['settings', 'settings', 'Settings'], ['help', 'help', 'Help']]; return `<nav class="activity-rail" aria-label="Workspace tools">${items.map(([id, ic, l]) => railBtn(id, ic, l)).join('')}<div class="rail-spacer"></div>${bottom.map(([id, ic, l]) => railBtn(id, ic, l)).join('')}</nav>`; }
    function railBtn(id, ic, label) { return `<button class="rail-btn ${state.activePanel === id ? 'active' : ''}" data-panel="${id}" aria-label="${label}" title="${label}">${icon(ic)}</button>`; }
    function renderLeftPanel() { const titles = { files: 'Files', outline: 'Outline', search: 'Search', insert: 'Insert', assets: 'Media', references: 'References', diagnostics: 'Publishing checks' }; const title = titles[state.activePanel] || state.activePanel; return `<aside class="left-panel"><div class="panel-head"><button class="icon-btn mobile-panel-back" data-action="close-panel" aria-label="Close panel">${icon('close')}</button><span class="panel-title">${escapeHtml(title)}

    function effectivePageSize() { return effectivePageSizeFor('inherit'); }
    function layoutViewportWidth() {
        return Number(document.documentElement?.clientWidth || window.innerWidth || 1024);

    function isMobilePreviewViewport() {
        // Breakpoints must follow the layout viewport. visualViewport.width changes during
        // pinch-zoom and would otherwise make Manuscript counter-scale the browser zoom.
        return layoutViewportWidth() <= 767;
    }
    function mobilePreviewAvailableWidth() {
        const preview = $('#preview-scroll');
        const layout = layoutViewportWidth();
        const local = Number(preview?.getBoundingClientRect?.().width || preview?.clientWidth || layout);
        let horizontalPadding = 0;
        try {
            const computed = preview ? getComputedStyle(preview) : null;
            horizontalPadding = (parseFloat(computed?.paddingLeft || '0') || 0) + (parseFloat(computed?.paddingRight || '0') || 0);
        }
        catch { }
        return Math.max(220, Math.min(layout, local) - horizontalPadding - 24);
    }
    function pagePreviewFitScale(pages = state.paginationResult?.pages || []) {
        const base = effectivePageSize();
        const widestMm = Math.max(base.width, ...pages.map(page => Number(page?.widthMm) || 0));
        const pagePx = Math.max(1, widestMm * (96 / 25.4));
        return clamp(mobilePreviewAvailableWidth() / pagePx, .2, 1);
    }
    function applyPagePreviewScale(wrap = $('#pages-wrap'), pages = state.paginationResult?.pages || []) {

    function jumpToPage(pageNumber, behavior = 'smooth') {
        const total = state.paginationResult?.pages?.length || 0;
        const requested = Math.round(Number(pageNumber) || 1);
        const targetIndex = Math.max(1, Math.min(total || 1, requested));
        const page = $(`#pages-wrap .page[data-page-index="${targetIndex}"]`);
        if (!page) return false;
        state.currentPage = targetIndex;
        page.scrollIntoView({ behavior, block: 'start', inline: 'center' });
        updatePagePositionUi();
        return true;
    }

    function updateCurrentPageFromScroll() {
        if (state.previewMode !== 'pages') return;
        if (pageScrollFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(pageScrollFrame);
        pageScrollFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            pageScrollFrame = 0;
            const scroll = $('#preview-scroll');
            const pages = [...$$('#pages-wrap .page')];
            if (!scroll || !pages.length) return;
            const center = scroll.getBoundingClientRect().top + Math.min(scroll.clientHeight * .42, 320);
            let best = pages[0], distance = Infinity;
            for (const page of pages) {
                const rect = page.getBoundingClientRect();
                const d = Math.abs((rect.top + Math.min(rect.height * .18, 100)) - center);
                if (d < distance) { distance = d; best = page; }
            }
            const next = Number(best.dataset.pageIndex || 1);
            if (next !== state.currentPage) { state.currentPage = next; updatePagePositionUi(); }
        });
    }
